## 📦 Thorn: ADMAnalysis<a id='admanalysis'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **ADMAnalysis::normalize_dtheta_dphi** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Project angular components onto r*dtheta and r*sin(theta)*dphi? <br> *Range:* `Project angular components onto r*dtheta and r*sin(theta)*dphi?` |
| **ADMAnalysis::ricci_persist** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Keep storage of  the  Ricci tensor and scalar around? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **ADMAnalysis::ricci_prolongation_type** | `KEYWORD` | `none` | The kind of boundary prolongation for the Ricci tensor and scalar <br> *Range:* `no prolongation (use this if you do not have enough time levels active)` |
| **ADMAnalysis::ricci_timelevels** | `INT` | `1` | Number of time levels for the Ricci tensor and scalar <br> *Range:* `1:3` |

## 📦 Thorn: ADMBaseX<a id='admbasex'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **ADMBaseX::initial_data** | `KEYWORD` | `Cartesian Minkowski` | Initial metric and extrinsic curvature datasets <br> *Range:* `linear wave` |
| **ADMBaseX::initial_dtlapse** | `KEYWORD` | `zero` | Initial dtlapse value <br> *Range:* `Dtlapse is zero` |
| **ADMBaseX::initial_dtshift** | `KEYWORD` | `zero` | Initial dtshift value <br> *Range:* `Dtshift is zero` |
| **ADMBaseX::initial_lapse** | `KEYWORD` | `one` | Initial lapse value <br> *Range:* `Uniform lapse` |
| **ADMBaseX::initial_shift** | `KEYWORD` | `zero` | Initial shift value <br> *Range:* `Shift is zero` |
| **ADMBaseX::linear_wave_amplitude** | `REAL` | `1.0e-8` | Linear wave amplitude <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |
| **ADMBaseX::linear_wave_wavelength** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Linear wave wavelength <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |
| **ADMBaseX::noise_amplitude** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Noise amplitude <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |

## 📦 Thorn: ADMBase<a id='admbase'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **ADMBase::admbase_boundary_condition** | `STRING` | `flat` | Boundary condition for ADMBase variables <br> *Range:* `,` |
| **ADMBase::dtlapse_evolution_method** | `KEYWORD` | `static` | The dtlapse evolution method <br> *Range:* `dtlapse is not evolved and initial data is used to fill in new grid points before each step and after grid changes` |
| **ADMBase::dtshift_evolution_method** | `KEYWORD` | `static` | The dtshift evolution method <br> *Range:* `dtshift is not evolved and initial data is used to fill in new grid points before each step and after grid changes` |
| **ADMBase::evolution_method** | `KEYWORD` | `static` | The metric an extrinsic curvature evolution method <br> *Range:* `The metric and extrinsic curvature are not evolved and initial data is used to fill in new grid points before each step and after grid changes` |
| **ADMBase::initial_data** | `KEYWORD` | `Cartesian Minkowski` | Initial metric and extrinsic curvature datasets <br> *Range:* `Minkowski values in cartesian coordinates` |
| **ADMBase::initial_dtlapse** | `KEYWORD` | `none` | Initial dtlapse value <br> *Range:* `Dtlapse is zero` |
| **ADMBase::initial_dtshift** | `KEYWORD` | `none` | Initial dtshift value <br> *Range:* `Dtshift is zero` |
| **ADMBase::initial_lapse** | `KEYWORD` | `one` | Initial lapse value <br> *Range:* `Uniform lapse` |
| **ADMBase::initial_shift** | `KEYWORD` | `zero` | Initial shift value <br> *Range:* `Shift is zero` |
| **ADMBase::lapse_evolution_method** | `KEYWORD` | `static` | The lapse evolution method <br> *Range:* `lapse is not evolved and initial data is used to fill in new grid points before each step and after grid changes` |
| **ADMBase::lapse_prolongation_type** | `KEYWORD` | `Lagrange` | The kind of boundary prolongation for the lapse <br> *Range:* `no prolongation (use this if you do not have enough time levels active)` |
| **ADMBase::lapse_timelevels** | `INT` | `1` | Number of time levels for the lapse <br> *Range:* `0:3` |
| **ADMBase::metric_prolongation_type** | `KEYWORD` | `Lagrange` | The kind of boundary prolongation for the metric and extrinsic curvature <br> *Range:* `no prolongation (use this if you do not have enough time levels active)` |
| **ADMBase::metric_timelevels** | `INT` | `1` | Number of time levels for the metric and extrinsic curvature <br> *Range:* `0:3` |
| **ADMBase::metric_type** | `KEYWORD` | `physical` | The semantics of the metric variables (physical, static conformal, etc) <br> *Range:* `metric and extrinsic curvature are the physical ones` |
| **ADMBase::shift_evolution_method** | `KEYWORD` | `static` | The shift evolution method <br> *Range:* `shift is not evolved and initial data is used to fill in new grid points before each step and after grid changes` |
| **ADMBase::shift_prolongation_type** | `KEYWORD` | `Lagrange` | The kind of boundary prolongation for the shift <br> *Range:* `no prolongation (use this if you do not have enough time levels active)` |
| **ADMBase::shift_timelevels** | `INT` | `1` | Number of time levels for the shift <br> *Range:* `0:3` |

## 📦 Thorn: ADMDerivatives<a id='admderivatives'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **ADMDerivatives::spatial_deriv_order** | `INT` | `-1` | Order of spatial derivatives <br> *Range:* `2:8` |
| **ADMDerivatives::store_cartesian_derivatives** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Switch calculation of cartesian derivatives of ADM lapse, shift, 3-metric on/off <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **ADMDerivatives::store_radial_derivatives** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Switch calculation of radial derivatives of ADM lapse, shift, 3-metric on/off <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **ADMDerivatives::store_time_derivatives** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Switch calculation of time derivatives of ADM lapse, shift, 3-metric on/off <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **ADMDerivatives::synchronize** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | switch off synchronization -- experimental <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **ADMDerivatives::timelevels** | `INT` | `1` | Number of timelevels for all variables <br> *Range:* `Useful for time interpolation` |

## 📦 Thorn: ADMMass<a id='admmass'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass_Debug** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Enable some info at runtime <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass_Excise_Horizons** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should we exclude the region inside the AH to the volume integral <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass_distance_from_grid_boundary** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | distance between the grid boundaries and the surface of the integration box <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass_number** | `INT` | `1` | number of measurements <br> *Range:* `0:` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass_surface_distance** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | distance between the above-defined center of the integration (cubic) box and its surface <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass_use_all_volume_as_volume_radius** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use the whole grid for volume integration <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass_use_surface_distance_as_volume_radius** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Use ADMMass\_surface\_distance instead of ADMMass\_volume\_radius <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass_volume_radius** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | radius of the sphere inside which the volume integral is computed <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass_x_max** | `REAL` | `100.0` | x position of the righttmost yz-plane for the integration box <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass_x_min** | `REAL` | `-100.0` | x position of the leftmost yz-plane for the integration box <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass_x_pos** | `REAL` | `0.0` | x position of the center of the integration box <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **ADMMass::ADMMass_y_max** | `REAL` | `100.0` | y position of the rightmost xz-plane for the integration box <br> *Range:* `:` |

| **AHFinderDirect::Jacobian_perturbation_amplitude** | `REAL` | `1.0e-6` | perturbation amplitude for 1-sided finite differencing for Jacobians <br> *Range:* `(0.0:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::Jacobian_store_solve_method** | `KEYWORD` | `row-oriented sparse matrix/UMFPACK` | how do we store/linear-solve the Jacobian matrix? <br> *Range:* `store as sparse matrix (row-oriented storage format),    solve with UMFPACK (sparse LU decomposition) method` |
| **AHFinderDirect::N_horizons** | `INT` | `1` | number of apparent horizons to search for <br> *Range:* `1:100` |
| **AHFinderDirect::N_zones_per_right_angle** | `INT` | `18` | sets angular resolution of patch systems <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::OpenDX_control_file_name_extension** | `STRING` | `dx` | file name extension for OpenDX control files <br> *Range:* `any nonempty string` |

| **AHFinderDirect::check_that_geometry_is_finite** | `BOOLEAN` | `true` | should we check the interpolated geometry variables are finite? <br> *Range:* `true` |

| **AHFinderDirect::surface_interpolator_pars** | `STRING` | `order=2    boundary_off_centering_tolerance={1.0e-10 1.0e-10 1.0e-10 1.0e-10}    boundary_extrapolation_tolerance={0.0 0.0 0.0 0.0}` | parameters for the surface interpolator <br> *Range:* `.*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::surface_modification** | `KEYWORD` | `none` | how to modify the surface definition <br> *Range:* `multiply with the areal radius (does not converge nicely, because the Jacobian is only approximate)` |
| **AHFinderDirect::surface_selection** | `KEYWORD` | `definition` | search for a surface with this areal radius <br> *Range:* `look for a surface with a certain product of expansion and areal radius` |
| **AHFinderDirect::test_all_Jacobian_compute_methods** | `BOOLEAN` | `true` | should we test all Jacobian computation methods, or just NP? <br> *Range:* `true` |
| **AHFinderDirect::track_origin_from_grid_scalar** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | track horizon origin from given grid scalars <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **AHFinderDirect::use_pretracking** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | search for an outermost apparent horizon <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **AHFinderDirect::verbose_level** | `KEYWORD` | `physics details` | controls which (how many) messages to print describing AH finding <br> *Range:* `physics details + lots and lots of messages about the AH-finding algorithm` |
| **AHFinderDirect::want_expansion_gradients** | `BOOLEAN` | `false` | should we print the gradients of the expansions? <br> *Range:* `false` |
| **AHFinderDirect::warn_level__gij_not_positive_definite__initial** | `INT` | `2` | warning level if the interpolated g\_{ij} isn't positive definite    (usually this means we're too close to a singularity)    (error occurs on first Newton iteration) <br> *Range:* `-1:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::warn_level__gij_not_positive_definite__subsequent** | `INT` | `2` | warning level if the interpolated g\_{ij} isn't positive definite    (usually this means we're too close to a singularity)    (error occurs on subsequent Newton iteration) <br> *Range:* `-1:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::warn_level__nonfinite_geometry** | `INT` | `1` | warning level if we find infinity and/or NaN in the interpolated geometry    values {g\_ij, partial\_k g\_ij, K\_ij} <br> *Range:* `-1:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::warn_level__point_outside__initial** | `INT` | `1` | warning level for point outside (or too close to boundary of) Cactus grid    (error occurs on first Newton iteration) <br> *Range:* `-1:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::warn_level__point_outside__subsequent** | `INT` | `2` | warning level for point outside (or too close to boundary of) Cactus grid    (error occurs on subsequent Newton iteration) <br> *Range:* `-1:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::warn_level__skipping_finite_check** | `INT` | `3` | warning level if the user sets  check\_that\_geometry\_is\_finite    but the Cactus configure process doesn't find a  finite()  function    so we have to skip the finite-geometry check <br> *Range:* `-1:*` |
| **AHFinderDirect::which_horizon_to_announce_centroid** | `INT` | `0` | for which horizon should we announce the centroid? <br> *Range:* `1:100` |
| **AHFinderDirect::which_surface_to_store_info** | `INT` | `-1` | into which surface should we store the info? <br> *Range:* `0:*` |

| **AHFinder::ahf_ReportAlways** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Report for all surfaces found (yes) or just for apparent horizons (no) <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_areamap** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Find area map? <br> *Range:* `no` |

| **AHFinder::ahf_flowh** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Weight of H flow <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_flowiter** | `INT` | `200` | Maximum number of iterations for flow <br> *Range:* `0:` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_flown** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Weight of N flow <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_flowtol** | `REAL` | `0.0001` | Tolerance for flow <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_gaussout** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Output gaussian curvature of horizon? <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_guess_absmin** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use absolute min to start minimization? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_guessold** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use old horizon as initial guess? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_guessverbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Print info on initial guess? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_inner** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Look for inner horizon? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_l0_guess** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Manual guess for l=0 component <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_l10_guess** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Manual guess for l=10 component <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_l12_guess** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Manual guess for l=12 component <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_l14_guess** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Manual guess for l=14 component <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_l16_guess** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Manual guess for l=16 component <br> *Range:* `:` |

| **AHFinder::ahf_mask** | `KEYWORD` | `off` | Use mask? <br> *Range:* `Mask set for both definite and probable horizons` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_mask_0** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Mask for first horizon with find3? <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_mask_1** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Mask for second horizon with find3? <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_mask_2** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Mask for third horizon with find3? <br> *Range:* `yes` |

| **AHFinder::ahf_maskshrink** | `REAL` | `0.8` | Shrink factor for mask <br> *Range:* `0.0:1.0` |

| **AHFinder::ahf_minarea** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Minimize area instead of expansion? <br> *Range:* `no` |

| **AHFinder::ahf_ntheta** | `INT` | `100` | Number of subdivisions in theta <br> *Range:* `1:` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_octant** | `KEYWORD` | `no` | Octant symmetry? <br> *Range:* `Octant symmetry + symmetry of rotation of pi/2 around z axis` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_offset** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Center offset from origin? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_phi** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Expand also in phi? (seach for non-axisymmetric surface) <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_r0** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Radius of initial sphere (0 forces largest sphere) <br> *Range:* `:` |

| **AHFinder::ahf_r0_1** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Radius of second initial sphere for find3 (0 forces largest sphere) <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_r0_2** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Radius of third initial sphere for find3 (0 forces largest sphere) <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_refx** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Reflection symmetry x->-x? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_refy** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Reflection symmetry y->-y? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_refz** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Reflection symmetry z->-z? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_shiftcoeff** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Coefficient for shift <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_sloppyguess** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use sphere as initial guess? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_tol** | `REAL` | `0.1` | Tolerance for minimization routines <br> *Range:* `0:` |
| **AHFinder::ahf_trapped_surface** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Minimize (expansion + delta) to find trapped surface? <br> *Range:* `no` |

| **AHFinder::ahf_wander** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Allow the center to wander? <br> *Range:* `no` |

| **AHFinder::ahf_yc_2** | `REAL` | `0.0` | y-coordinate of center of expansion for third surface with find3 <br> *Range:* `:` |

## 📦 Thorn: Cactus<a id='cactus'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **Cactus::cctk_final_time** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | Final time for evolution <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **Cactus::cctk_initial_time** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Initial time for evolution <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **Cactus::cctk_itlast** | `INT` | `10` | Final iteration number <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **Cactus::max_runtime** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Terminate evolution loop after a certain elapsed runtime (in minutes) <br> *Range:* `0.0:` |
| **Cactus::presync_mode** | `KEYWORD` | `off` | behavior of the automatic pre-synchronization of grid fuctions <br> *Range:* `The driver ignores SYNC statements and requires READS or WRITES for all variable access` |
| **Cactus::terminate** | `KEYWORD` | `iteration` | Condition on which to terminate evolution loop <br> *Range:* `Take termination condition from both iteration number and coordinate time (DEPRECATED IN BETA14)` |
| **Cactus::terminate_next** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Terminate on next iteration? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Cactus::allow_mixeddim_gfs** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Allow use of GFs from different dimensions <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Cactus::cctk_brief_output** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Give only brief output <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Cactus::cctk_full_warnings** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Give detailed information for each warning statement <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Cactus::cctk_show_banners** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Show any registered banners for the different thorns <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **Cactus::cctk_show_schedule** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Print the scheduling tree to standard output <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **Cactus::cctk_strong_param_check** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Die on parameter errors in CCTK\_PARAMCHECK <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **Cactus::cctk_timer_output** | `KEYWORD` | `off` | Provide output from timers <br> *Range:* `Detailed timer output` |
| **Cactus::highlight_warning_messages** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Highlight CCTK warning messages ? <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **Cactus::info_format** | `KEYWORD` | `basic` | What information do CCTK\_INFO() and CCTK\_VInfo() print? <br> *Range:* `numeric_timestamp\thuman readable timestamp: INFO (ThornName): message` |
| **Cactus::recovery_mode** | `KEYWORD` | `strict` | How to behave when recovering from a checkpoint <br> *Range:* `Recover variables if possible, after INITIAL timebins have been scheduled` |
| **Cactus::schedule_sort_mode** | `KEYWORD` | `none` | How to behave when no explicit ordering is prescribed between itemse <br> *Range:* `sorted in reverse alphabetical order by thorn name and item name` |
| **Cactus::schedule_sort_warnings** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Output warnings if a schedule item refers to a non-existing item for ordering <br> *Range:* `no` |

| **CanudaX_BSSNMoL::derivs_order** | `INT` | `4` | Accuracy of spatial finite difference stencils in RHS calculation <br> *Range:* `8th order accurate stencils` |
| **CanudaX_BSSNMoL::diss_eps** | `REAL` | `0.15` | Strength coefficient for Kreiss-Oliger dissipation <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |
| **CanudaX_BSSNMoL::diss_order** | `INT` | `5` | Accuracy of Kreiss-Oliger dissipation stencils; should be 1 highehr than derivs\_order <br> *Range:* `N/A` |
| **CanudaX_BSSNMoL::eta_beta** | `REAL` | `1` | Damping parameter in live shift <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **CanudaX_BSSNMoL::eta_transition** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use an r-dependent damping parameter eta? <br> *Range:* `no` |

| **CanudaX_BSSNMoL::eta_transition_r** | `REAL` | `1` | Transition radius for r-dependent eta <br> *Range:* `0:*` |

| **CanudaX_BSSNMoL::make_aa_tracefree** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Remove trace of \tilde{A}\_{ij} after each timestep? <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **CanudaX_BSSNMoL::n_aij** | `INT` | `2` | n power of outgoing boundary r^n fall off rate for conformal tracefree extrinsic curvature <br> *Range:* `0:2` |

| **CanudaX_BSSNMoL::n_trk** | `INT` | `2` | n power of outgoing boundary r^n fall off rate for trace of extrinsic curvature <br> *Range:* `0:2` |

| **CanudaX_BSSNMoL::refinement_criterion** | `KEYWORD` | `fixed` | How to compute regrid error for AMR? <br> *Range:* `Sum absolute values of each term in Hamiltonian constraint` |
| **CanudaX_BSSNMoL::rescale_shift_initial** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Initialize shift as psi^{-2} beta ? <br> *Range:* `no` |

## 📦 Thorn: CarpetLib<a id='carpetlib'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **CarpetLib::deadbeef** | `INT` | `666` | A strange integer value that indicates that something has gone wrong; the integer equivalent of a nan <br> *Range:* `N/A` |
| **CarpetLib::poison_value** | `INT` | `255` | Integer value (0..255) used to poison new timelevels (with memset) <br> *Range:* `0:255` |
| **CarpetLib::restriction_order_space** | `INT` | `3` | Order of restriction operator to use with use\_higher\_order\_restriction <br> *Range:* `fifth order accurate restriction for grid functions where prolongation is not (W)ENO` |
| **CarpetLib::support_staggered_operators** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Provide one extra ghost point during restriction for staggered operators - EXPERIMENTAL <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::use_higher_order_restriction** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use third order cell centered restriction operators instead of first order <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::barrier_between_stages** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Add a barrier between the communication stages (slows down, but may make timing numbers easier to interpret) <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::barriers** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Insert barriers at strategic places for debugging purposes (slows down execution) <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::check_bboxes** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Check bounding box information for self-consistency <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **CarpetLib::check_communication_schedule** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Check the communication schedule at run time (expensive) <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::combine_recompose** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Recompose all grid functions of one refinement levels at once <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **CarpetLib::combine_sends** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Send data together and in order of processor ranks <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::commstate_verbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Print debug info from the commstate class <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::electric_fence** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Surround each allocated memory block by canaries to check for out-of-bounds accesses <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::fence_width** | `INT` | `1` | number of guard cells to use <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **CarpetLib::interleave_communications** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Try to interleave communications with each other; each processor begins to communicate with its 'right neighbour' in rank, instead of with the root processor <br> *Range:* `N/A` |
| **CarpetLib::interpolate_from_buffer_zones** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use buffer points for interpolation <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::max_allowed_memory_MB** | `INT` | `0` | Maximum allowed amount of memory per process that can be allocated for grid variables (in Megabytes) <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **CarpetLib::max_core_size_MB** | `INT` | `-2` | Maximum size of a core file, set via setrlimit <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **CarpetLib::max_memory_size_MB** | `INT` | `-2` | Maximum amount of memory per MPI process, set via setrlimit <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **CarpetLib::memstat_file** | `STRING` | `carpetlib-memory-statistics` | File name in which memstat output is collected (because stdout from the root node may not be enough) <br> *Range:* `file name` |
| **CarpetLib::message_count_multiplier** | `INT` | `1` | Transmit messages this many times <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **CarpetLib::message_size_multiplier** | `INT` | `1` | Enlarge size of transmitted messages by this factor <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **CarpetLib::output_bboxes** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Output bounding box information to the screen <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::pad_to_cachelines** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Pad arrays to the cache line size (only when VECTORISE\_ALIGNED\_ARRAYS is set) DEPRECARED <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **CarpetLib::poison_new_memory** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Try to catch uninitialised data by setting newly allocated memory to values that will catch your attention <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::print_memstats_every** | `INT` | `-1` | Report periodically how much memory is used per process <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **CarpetLib::print_timestats_every** | `INT` | `-1` | Print timing statistics periodically <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **CarpetLib::test_backtrace** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Kill yourself to test the backtrace mechanism <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::timestat_file** | `STRING` | `carpetlib-timing-statistics` | File name in which timestat output is collected (because stdout from the root node may not be enough) <br> *Range:* `file name` |
| **CarpetLib::use_dgfe** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use DGFE operators instead of Lagrange operators <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::use_ipm_timing_regions** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Call IPM (via MPI\_Pcontrol) to define regions <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::use_loopcontrol_in_operators** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use LoopControl to parallelize AMR operators <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::use_mpi_send** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use MPI\_Send instead of MPI\_Isend <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::use_mpi_ssend** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use MPI\_Ssend instead of MPI\_Isend <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **CarpetLib::use_openmp** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Use OpenMP to parallelize AMR operators <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **CarpetLib::verbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Print info to the screen <br> *Range:* `no` |

| **Carpet::split_components** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Split components onto processes; without this, one needs many components and few processes <br> *Range:* `N/A` |
| **Carpet::split_direction** | `INT` | `2` | Direction in which the domain should be split (for processor\_topology=along-dir) <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **Carpet::split_interior_points_x** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Uniformly divide interior points across processors in x-direction <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Carpet::split_interior_points_y** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Uniformly divide interior points across processors in y-direction <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Carpet::split_interior_points_z** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Uniformly divide interior points across processors in z-direction <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Carpet::storage_verbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Display verbose storage information if veryverbose <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Carpet::suppress_restriction** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Suppress the restriction operations.  This makes the coarser refinement levels independent of the finer ones. <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Carpet::symmetrically_distribute_points_x** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Symmetrically distribute points in x-direction over domain center <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Carpet::symmetrically_distribute_points_y** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Symmetrically distribute points in y-direction over domain center <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Carpet::symmetrically_distribute_points_z** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Symmetrically distribute points in z-direction over domain center <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Carpet::sync_barriers** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Insert barriers before and after syncs, so that the sync timer is more reliable (slows down execution) <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Carpet::sync_during_time_integration** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Synchronise during time integration, even when prolongation is switched off <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **Carpet::time_interpolation_during_regridding** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Interpolate finer levels in time during regridding <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **Carpet::timer_file** | `STRING` | `carpet-timing-statistics` | File name in which detailed timing statistics are collected <br> *Range:* `file name` |
| **Carpet::timing_average_window_minutes** | `REAL` | `10.0` | Time interval (in wall time minutes) for calculating the current physics time per hour <br> *Range:* `(0.0:*` |
| **Carpet::use_buffer_zones** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use buffer zones <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Carpet::use_overlap_zones** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use overlap zones <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Carpet::use_tapered_grids** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use tapered grids, avoiding time interpolation during evolution <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Carpet::use_unusedpoints_mask** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Turn on storage and usage of 'unusedpoints\_mask' <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Carpet::verbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Display more info on the screen <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Carpet::veryverbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Display a lot of info on the screen <br> *Range:* `no` |

| **Coordinates::shiftout_outer_boundaries** | `INT` | `0` | Offset between the boundary location and the first outer boundary point <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **Coordinates::sphere_inner_radius** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Inner radius for the spherical grids <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::sphere_medium_radius** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Medium radius for the 13 patch system spherical grids <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::sphere_outer_radius** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Location of the physical outer boundary. <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::stagger_outer_boundaries** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Stagger the grid at the outer boundaries <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Coordinates::stagger_patch_boundaries** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Stagger the grid at the inter-patch boundaries? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Coordinates::store_inverse_jacobian** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Numerically evaluate and store the transformation dx^i/da^k  (a: local, x: global) <br> *Range:* `Numerically evaluate and store the transformation dx^i/da^k  (a: local, x: global)` |
| **Coordinates::store_jacobian** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Numerically evaluate and store the transformation da^i/dx^k  (a: local, x: global) <br> *Range:* `Numerically evaluate and store the transformation da^i/dx^k  (a: local, x: global)` |
| **Coordinates::store_jacobian_derivative** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Store the derivative of the Jacobian d^2[global]/d[local]^2 <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **Coordinates::store_volume_form** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Store determinant of Jacobian <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Coordinates::stretch_rmax_1** | `REAL` | `2e10` | Outer radius of first stretching region <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::stretch_rmin_1** | `REAL` | `1e10` | Inner radius of first stretching region <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **Coordinates::symmetry** | `KEYWORD` | `full` | Select a symmetry <br> *Range:* `octant mode (positive xyz)` |
| **Coordinates::theta_min** | `REAL` | `10` | Minimal polar angle to cover by the spherical grid patch, in degrees <br> *Range:* `(0:90)` |
| **Coordinates::transition_radius** | `REAL` | `2.0` | Transition radius between box and cylinder <br> *Range:* `(0:*` |
| **Coordinates::verbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Output information periodically <br> *Range:* `yes` |

| **Dissipation::extra_dissipation_at_outerbound** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | increase dissipation at outer boundary <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Dissipation::extra_dissipation_in_horizons** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | extra dissipation in horizons <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Dissipation::horizon_number** | `INT` | `-1` | horizon number for extra dissipation in horizons -- AHFinderDirect number <br> *Range:* `1:*` |

| **Dissipation::use_dissipation_near_excision** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Apply excision near the excision boundary (does not work for high orders) <br> *Range:* `yes` |

## 📦 Thorn: EHFinder<a id='ehfinder'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **EHFinder::EHFinder_MaxNumEvolvedVars** | `INT` | `1` | The maximum number of evolved variables used by EHFinder <br> *Range:* `1:10` |
| **EHFinder::EHFinder_Max_Evolved_Array_Size** | `INT` | `1` | The maximum size of evolved grid arrays used by EHFinder <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::EHFinder_Num_ArrayEvolved_Vars** | `INT` | `3` | The maximum number of evolved grid arrays used by EHFinder <br> *Range:* `0:30` |
| **EHFinder::area_calculation_method** | `KEYWORD` | `standard` | How should the areas be calculated <br> *Range:* `Using an isosurface triangulation` |
| **EHFinder::area_interpolation_order** | `INT` | `3` | What order should be used for the area interpoation <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::area_interpolator** | `STRING` | `Lagrange polynomial interpolation` | What interpolator should be used for the area <br> *Range:* `A valid interpolator name` |
| **EHFinder::cas_a** | `REAL` | `2.0` | Initial a coefficient of ovaloid of cassini <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **EHFinder::cas_b** | `REAL` | `2.05` | Initial b coefficient of ovaloid of cassini <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **EHFinder::center_x** | `REAL` | `0.0` | The x-coordinate of the center <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::center_y** | `REAL` | `0.0` | The y-coordinate of the center <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::center_z** | `REAL` | `0.0` | The z-coordinate of the center <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::cheat** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should we cheat and evolve using the last data set for a while? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **EHFinder::cheat_iterations** | `INT` | `0` | For how many iterations should we cheat <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **EHFinder::eh_lapse_type** | `KEYWORD` | `numerical` | Do we use numerical or analytic metric information <br> *Range:* `Use external analytic metric` |
| **EHFinder::eh_metric_type** | `KEYWORD` | `numerical` | Do we use numerical or analytic metric information <br> *Range:* `Use external analytic metric` |
| **EHFinder::eh_number_level_sets** | `INT` | `1` | How many level set functions should we evolve <br> *Range:* `1:10` |
| **EHFinder::eh_shift_type** | `KEYWORD` | `numerical` | Do we use numerical or analytic metric information <br> *Range:* `Use external analytic metric` |
| **EHFinder::evolve_generators** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should the generators be evolved <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **EHFinder::file_type** | `KEYWORD` | `one_file` | Are the timesteps in separate files or in one file? <br> *Range:* `Timesteps are in separete files` |
| **EHFinder::generator_distribution** | `KEYWORD` | `line` | What initial distribution should be used <br> *Range:* `Put the generators on a surface with spherical topology` |
| **EHFinder::generator_interpolation_order** | `INT` | `3` | What order should be used for the generator interpoation <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::generator_interpolator** | `STRING` | `Lagrange polynomial interpolation` | What interpolator should be used for the generators <br> *Range:* `A valid interpolator name` |
| **EHFinder::generator_tracking_method** | `KEYWORD` | `interpolate_before` | What method should be used for tracking the generators <br> *Range:* `Calculate first, then interpolate` |
| **EHFinder::initial_a** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Initial a coefficient of ellipsoid <br> *Range:* `0.0:` |
| **EHFinder::initial_b** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Initial b coefficient of ellipsoid <br> *Range:* `0.0:` |
| **EHFinder::initial_c** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Initial c coefficient of ellipsoid <br> *Range:* `0.0:` |
| **EHFinder::initial_f** | `KEYWORD` | `sphere` | Initial surface choice <br> *Range:* `ovaloid of cassini` |
| **EHFinder::initial_rad** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Initial radius of surface <br> *Range:* `0.0:` |
| **EHFinder::last_iteration_number** | `INT` | `0` | The starting iteration number for the EH\_Finder (last iteration number of the simulation) <br> *Range:* `0:` |
| **EHFinder::maximum_surface_number** | `INT` | `1` | The maximum number of surfaces expected in the data <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::mode** | `KEYWORD` | `normal` | Mode of operation <br> *Range:* `Provide storage for f and initialize without evolving` |
| **EHFinder::n_array_ghosts** | `INT` | `1` | Number of ghost points in the surface grid array <br> *Range:* `1:` |
| **EHFinder::nphi** | `INT` | `51` | Number of points in the phi direction when finding points on the surface <br> *Range:* `1:*:2` |
| **EHFinder::ntheta** | `INT` | `51` | Number of points in the theta direction when finding points on the surface <br> *Range:* `1:*:2` |
| **EHFinder::number_of_generators** | `INT` | `1` | How many generators should be evolved <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::number_of_generators_phi** | `INT` | `1` | How many generators in the phi direction <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::number_of_generators_theta** | `INT` | `1` | How many generators in the theta direction <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::pde_differences** | `KEYWORD` | `upwind2` | Type of finite diffencing used in pde re-initialization <br> *Range:* `Use 2nd order upwinded differences everywhere` |
| **EHFinder::re_init_int_method** | `KEYWORD` | `euler` | Integration method used in re-initialization <br> *Range:* `Second order Runge-Kutta scheme` |
| **EHFinder::re_init_max_iter** | `INT` | `800` | maximum number of iterations in the re-initialization <br> *Range:* `0:` |
| **EHFinder::re_init_undo** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Should re-initialization be undone at pinch-off <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **EHFinder::re_init_verbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should re-initialization be verbose? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **EHFinder::re_initialize_every** | `INT` | `10` | How often to re-initialize the level set function <br> *Range:* `0:` |
| **EHFinder::read_conformal_factor_once** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Should the conformal factor only be read once <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **EHFinder::rotation_alpha** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Rotation angle around z-axis of ellipsoid <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::rotation_beta** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Rotation angle around y-axis of ellipsoid <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::rotation_gamma** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Rotation angle around x-axis of ellipsoid <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::saved_iteration_every** | `INT` | `1` | How often was the numerical data saved <br> *Range:* `1:` |
| **EHFinder::shell_width** | `REAL` | `7.0` | Width of the evolution region in units of the grid spacing <br> *Range:* `0.0:` |
| **EHFinder::surface_direction** | `KEYWORD` | `outward` | Should we track outward or inward moving surfaces <br> *Range:* `Track inward moving surfaces.` |
| **EHFinder::surface_interpolation_order** | `INT` | `2` | What order should be used for the surface interpoation <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **EHFinder::surface_interpolator** | `STRING` | `Hermite polynomial interpolation` | What interpolator should be used to locate the surface <br> *Range:* `A valid interpolator name` |
| **EHFinder::translate_x** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Translation in x-direction <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::translate_y** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Translation in y-direction <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::translate_z** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Translation in z-direction <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **EHFinder::upwind_type** | `KEYWORD` | `characteristic` | Type of upwinding used in evolving the ehfinder equations <br> *Range:* `Use characteristic information` |
| **EHFinder::use_inner_excision** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Should inner excision be used? <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **EHFinder::use_outer_excision** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Should outer excision be used? <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **EHFinder::use_user_center** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should the user prescribed center be used <br> *Range:* `no` |

| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **EOS_Omni::barotropiceos_gammath** | `REAL` | `2.0` | thermal gamma for barotropic EOS <br> *Range:* `1.0:*` |
| **EOS_Omni::barotropiceos_read_table** | `BOOLEAN` | `No` | Read in barotropic EOS table? <br> *Range:* `No` |
| **EOS_Omni::barotropiceos_table_name** | `STRING` | `blah.asc` | table name for barotropic EOS (ASCII) <br> *Range:* `Can be anything` |
| **EOS_Omni::barotropiceos_use_thermal_gamma_law** | `BOOLEAN` | `Yes` | use an additional thermal gamma? <br> *Range:* `Yes` |
| **EOS_Omni::coldeos_read_table** | `BOOLEAN` | `No` | Read in cold EOS table? <br> *Range:* `No` |
| **EOS_Omni::coldeos_table_name** | `STRING` | `blah.asc` | table name for cold EOS (ASCII) <br> *Range:* `Can be anything` |
| **EOS_Omni::coldeos_use_thermal_gamma_law** | `BOOLEAN` | `Yes` | use an additional thermal gamma? <br> *Range:* `Yes` |
| **EOS_Omni::do_energy_shift** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | shift energies around? <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **EOS_Omni::dump_nuceos_table** | `BOOLEAN` | `No` | Dump table in ASCII at beginning <br> *Range:* `No` |
| **EOS_Omni::dump_nuceos_table_name** | `STRING` | `blah.asc` | nuceos dump table name (ASCII) <br> *Range:* `Can be anything` |
| **EOS_Omni::gl_gamma** | `REAL` | `2.0` | Adiabatic Index for gamma-law EOS <br> *Range:* `(0:*` |
| **EOS_Omni::gl_k** | `REAL` | `100.0` | Polytropic constant in c=G=Msun=1 for gamma-law EOS <br> *Range:* `(0:*` |
| **EOS_Omni::hybrid_gamma** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Adiabatic index of each piece <br> *Range:* `(0:*` |
| **EOS_Omni::hybrid_gamma1** | `REAL` | `1.325` | subnuclear adiabatic Index for hybrid EOS <br> *Range:* `(0:*` |
| **EOS_Omni::hybrid_gamma2** | `REAL` | `2.5` | subnuclear adiabatic Index for hybrid EOS <br> *Range:* `(0:*` |
| **EOS_Omni::hybrid_gamma_th** | `REAL` | `1.5` | Thermal gamma for hybrid EOS <br> *Range:* `(0:*` |
| **EOS_Omni::hybrid_k0** | `REAL` | `0.0` | K coefficent of the first piece <br> *Range:* `(0:*` |
| **EOS_Omni::hybrid_k1** | `REAL` | `0.4640517` | Polytropic constant in c=G=Msun=1 for hybrid EOS <br> *Range:* `(0:*` |
| **EOS_Omni::hybrid_rho** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Density values at separation points <br> *Range:* `(0:*` |
| **EOS_Omni::hybrid_rho_nuc** | `REAL` | `3.238607e-4` | Density at which to switch between gammas; c=G=Msun=1 <br> *Range:* `N/A` |
| **EOS_Omni::n_pieces** | `INT` | `2` | Number of polytropic pieces <br> *Range:* `1:10` |
| **EOS_Omni::nuceos_read_table** | `BOOLEAN` | `No` | Read in EOS table? <br> *Range:* `No` |
| **EOS_Omni::nuceos_table_name** | `STRING` | `blah.h5` | nuceos table name (hdf5) <br> *Range:* `Can be anything` |
| **EOS_Omni::poly_gamma** | `REAL` | `2.0` | Adiabatic Index for poly EOS <br> *Range:* `(0:*` |
| **EOS_Omni::poly_gamma_initial** | `REAL` | `-1` | Initial Adiabatic Index for poly and hybrid EOS <br> *Range:* `(0:*` |
| **EOS_Omni::poly_k** | `REAL` | `100.0` | Polytropic constant in c=G=Msun=1 <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **EOS_Omni::read_table_on_single_process** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | read table on one process only and bcast data <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **EOS_Omni::reader_process** | `INT` | `0` | read table on this process and bcast data <br> *Range:* `0:*` |

## 📦 Thorn: ET_BHaHAHA<a id='et_bhahaha'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_BBH_mode_common_horizon_idx** | `INT` | `2` | BBH mode: Horizon indices (zero-offset) for two inspiralling BHs. E.g., if the common horizon has index 2, then set bah\_BBH\_mode\_common\_horizon\_idx=2 <br> *Range:* `0:2` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_BBH_mode_enable** | `INT` | `0` | 0 = disable; 1 = enable <br> *Range:* `N/A` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_BBH_mode_inspiral_BH_idxs** | `INT` | `0` | BBH mode: Horizon indices (zero-offset) for two inspiralling BHs. E.g., if the inspiralling BHs have indices 0,1 then set BHaHAHA::bah\_BBH\_mode\_inspiral\_BH\_idxs = [0,1] <br> *Range:* `0:2` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_KO_strength** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Hyperbolic relaxation Kreiss-Oliger dissipation parameter. This usually hurts more than it helps, so is disabled (0.0) by default. <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_M_scale** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Mass scale for each horizon. <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_Nphi_array_multigrid** | `INT` | `-100` | Multigrid Nphi array. <br> *Range:* `2:*:2` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_Nr_interp_max** | `INT` | `48` | Maximum number of radial points for  grid(s): Number of points sampling radial direction. <br> *Range:* `4:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_Ntheta_array_multigrid** | `INT` | `-100` | Multigrid Ntheta array. <br> *Range:* `2:*:2` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_Theta_L2_times_M_tolerance** | `REAL` | `1e-2` | Primary convergence criterion: area-weighted L2 norm of residual function Theta on horizon. <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_Theta_Linf_times_M_tolerance** | `REAL` | `1e-5` | Secondary convergence criterion: L-infinity norm of Theta, time mass scale. <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_cfl_factor** | `REAL` | `0.98` | Courant-Friedrichs-Lewy (CFL) factor to set timestep within BHaHAHA hyperbolic relaxation. 0.98 robust default, even for new horizon formation (e.g., common horizons). <br> *Range:* `0:1.0` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_enable_eta_varying_alg_for_precision_common_horizon** | `INT` | `0` | Enable eta-varying algorithm for high-precision common horizon finding. For general-purpose horizon finding, leave this disabled. <br> *Range:* `0:1` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_eta_damping_times_M** | `REAL` | `7.0` | Hyperbolic relaxation exponential damping parameter. <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_initial_grid_x_center** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Initial x locations of horizon grid centers <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_initial_grid_y_center** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Initial y locations of horizon grid centers <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_initial_grid_z_center** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Initial z locations of horizon grid centers <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_max_iterations** | `INT` | `10000` | Maximum hyperbolic relaxation steps before ending the horizon search. <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_max_search_radius** | `REAL` | `1.5` | Input spherical grid(s): maximum radius to search, for all time. <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_num_resolutions_multigrid** | `INT` | `3` | Number of resolutions provided in bah\_N{theta,phi}\_array\_multigrid[] arrays <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::bah_verbosity_level** | `INT` | `1` | 0 = essential only; 1 = physical summary of found horizon; 2 = full algorithmic details <br> *Range:* `N/A` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::find_every** | `INT` | `1` | Search cadence for all horizons. <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::find_every_individual** | `INT` | `-1` | Search cadence for individual apparent horizons. (overrides find\_every if >= 1). <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::interpolator_name** | `STRING` | `Hermite polynomial interpolation` | Which interpolator should I use <br> *Range:* `Any nonempty string` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::interpolator_pars** | `STRING` | `order=3` | Parameters for the interpolator <br> *Range:* `.*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::max_Nphi** | `INT` | `64` | Maximum number of points that will sample the phi direction. For typical horizon finding, leave this to default value of 64. <br> *Range:* `2:256:2` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::max_Ntheta** | `INT` | `32` | Maximum number of points that will sample the theta direction. For typical horizon finding, leave this to default value of 32. <br> *Range:* `2:128:2` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::max_num_horizons** | `INT` | `3` | Maximum number of horizons for this simulation. <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::sync_data_across_ranks_after_each_search__warning_slow** | `INT` | `0` | Enables restart from a different number of MPI ranks. WARNING: Slows BHaHAHA by >2x <br> *Range:* `0:1` |
| **ET_BHaHAHA::which_surface_to_store_info** | `INT` | `-1` | Which SphericalSurface index should receive this horizon (-1 disables export). BHaHAHA horizon numbering is zero-based. On multi-rank runs, enabling SphericalSurface export forces a post-find sync and may carry the same cost as sync\_data\_across\_ranks\_after\_each\_search\_\_warning\_slow. <br> *Range:* `0:*` |

| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |

## 📦 Thorn: FLRWSolver<a id='flrwsolver'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW_boxlength** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Size of box used in comoving Mpc -- only used in generating power spectrum perturbations <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW_init_HL** | `REAL` | `1.0` | FLRW initial H\_* L -- ratio of box length to horizon size. This sets initial background density <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW_init_a** | `REAL` | `1.0` | FLRW initial scale factor <br> *Range:* `0.0001:*` |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW_lapse_value** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Value of background initial lapse (alpha) -- for perturbations this becomes alp=FLRW\_lapse\_value*sqrt(1 + 2 Phi) <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW_perturb** | `KEYWORD` | `no` | Add perturbation to matter and metric <br> *Range:* `Do not add perturbation, will run FLRW spacetime` |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW_perturb_direction** | `KEYWORD` | `all` | Choose which direction to perturb (only used if FLRW\_perturb\_type = single\_mode) <br> *Range:* `perturb all dimensions` |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW_perturb_type** | `KEYWORD` | `single_mode` | Add perturbation of the following type <br> *Range:* `powerspectrum_exact` |
| **FLRWSolver::FLRW_random_seed** | `INT` | `10` | Random seed for generating the Gaussian random density perturbation from the power spectrum given in FLRW\_powerspectrum\_file. <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **FLRWSolver::phi_amplitude** | `REAL` | `1.e-8` | Amplitude of perturbation in phi - implicitly sets amplitude of rho,vel perturbation <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |
| **FLRWSolver::phi_phase_offset** | `REAL` | `0.0` | The phase offset, eta, in phi=A*sin(kx*x - eta) for single mode perturbation -- applied same in all dimentions if FLRW\_perturb\_direction = all <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **FLRWSolver::single_perturb_wavelength** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Wavelength of single mode scalar perturbation relative to box size <br> *Range:* `0.0001:*` |

## 📦 Thorn: FishboneMoncriefID<a id='fishbonemoncriefid'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::A_b** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Scaling factor for the vector potential <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::M** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Kerr-Schild BH mass. Probably should always set M=1. <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::a** | `REAL` | `0.9375` | The spin parameter of the black hole <br> *Range:* `0:1.0` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::gamma** | `REAL` | `1.3333333333333333333333333333` | Equation of state: P = kappa * rho^gamma <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::kappa** | `REAL` | `1.0e-3` | Equation of state: P = kappa * rho^gamma <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::r_at_max_density** | `REAL` | `12.0` | Radius at maximum disk density. Needs to be > r\_in <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::r_in** | `REAL` | `6.0` | Fixes the inner edge of the disk <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::random_max** | `REAL` | `0.02` | Ceiling value of random perturbation to initial pressure, where perturbed pressure = pressure*(1.0 + (random\_min + (random\_max-random\_min)*RAND[0,1))) <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **FishboneMoncriefID::random_min** | `REAL` | `-0.02` | Floor value of random perturbation to initial pressure, where perturbed pressure = pressure*(1.0 + (random\_min + (random\_max-random\_min)*RAND[0,1))) <br> *Range:* `*:*` |

## 📦 Thorn: Formaline<a id='formaline'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **Formaline::announce_to_portal** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Announce meta information to the portal <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Formaline::collect_metadata** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Collect metadata and output it in various ways <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **Formaline::create_id_files** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Create files from the build and job ids <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **Formaline::max_warn_level** | `INT` | `-1` | Only output warnings with at most this level of severity <br> *Range:* `-1:*` |
| **Formaline::metadata_lifetime** | `INT` | `100` | Lifetime of meta information sent to a server (in hours) <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **Formaline::nr_of_parameter_changes_to_be_logged** | `INT` | `-1` | number of parameter changes to be logged <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **Formaline::out_reductions** | `STRING` | `minimum maximum norm1 norm2 norm_inf sum` | Reduction operators that should be applied to the output variables <br> *Range:* `,` |
| **Formaline::output_info** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Output info messages? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Formaline::output_source** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Output a tarball with the cactus source tree <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **Formaline::output_source_subdirectory** | `STRING` | `cactus-source` | Subdirectory, relative to IO::out\_dir, for the Cactus source tarballs <br> *Range:* `,` |
| **Formaline::portal_port** | `INT` | `9296` | Portal port <br> *Range:* `1:65535` |
| **Formaline::publish_level** | `INT` | `1` | Importance level for meta data to be published via the Publish API <br> *Range:* `0:10` |
| **Formaline::rdf_port** | `INT` | `9296` | RDF server port <br> *Range:* `1:65535` |
| **Formaline::send_as_rdf** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Send meta as RDF to a server <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **Formaline::steered_parameters_log_exclusion_list** | `STRING` | `CarpetRegrid::coordinates` | Parameters for which runtime changes should not be logged <br> *Range:* `CarpetRegrid::coordinates` |
| **Formaline::storage_filename** | `STRING` | `formaline-jar.txt` | File name for meta information <br> *Range:* `,` |
| **Formaline::storage_json_filename** | `STRING` | `formaline-jar.json` | File name for meta information in JSON format <br> *Range:* `,` |
| **Formaline::store_into_file** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Store meta information in a file <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **Formaline::store_into_json_file** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Store meta information in a file in JSON format <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **Formaline::timeout** | `INT` | `10` | Timeout for sending meta information to a server (in seconds) <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **Formaline::update_interval** | `REAL` | `3600.0` | Update interval for the meta information (in seconds) <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **Formaline::use_relay_host** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Use a relay host for connecting to the portal <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **Formaline::verbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Produce screen output <br> *Range:* `no` |

| **GRHayLID::ConstantDensitySphere_vy_interior** | `REAL` | `0` | Interior y-velocity <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **GRHayLID::ConstantDensitySphere_vz_interior** | `REAL` | `0` | Interior z-velocity <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **GRHayLID::IsotropicGas_Y_e** | `REAL` | `-1` | Gas electron fraction (dimensionless) <br> *Range:* `Forbidden value` |
| **GRHayLID::IsotropicGas_rho** | `REAL` | `-1` | Gas density in geometrized units <br> *Range:* `Forbidden value` |
| **GRHayLID::IsotropicGas_temperature** | `REAL` | `-1` | Gas temperature in MeV <br> *Range:* `Forbidden value` |
| **GRHayLID::beq_temperature** | `REAL` | `-1` | Temperature used to impose beta-equilibrium <br> *Range:* `Forbidden value` |
| **GRHayLID::discontinuity_position** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Set location of the shock discontinuity <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **GRHayLID::impose_beta_equilibrium** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Impose beta-equilibrium on initial data <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHayLID::initial_data_1D** | `KEYWORD` | `Balsara1` | Set initial data type for 1D tests <br> *Range:* `initial data for the Balsara5 test` |
| **GRHayLID::initialize_magnetic_quantities** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Set whether Avec should be initialized <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **GRHayLID::shock_direction** | `KEYWORD` | `x` | Set direction for shock in test data <br> *Range:* `Test data will have discontinuities along z direction` |
| **GRHayLID::stagger_A_fields** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Sets whether Avec is staggered to +1/2 (following IllinoisGRMHD convention) <br> *Range:* `yes` |

## 📦 Thorn: GRHayLib<a id='grhaylib'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **GRHayLib::Gamma** | `REAL` | `-1` | Gamma parameter <br> *Range:* `forbidden value to make sure it is explicitly set in the parfile` |
| **GRHayLib::Gamma_ppoly_in** | `REAL` | `-1` | Set polytropic Gamma parameters. Gamma\_ppoly\_in[0] goes from rho=0 to rho=rho\_ppoly\_in[0]. <br> *Range:* `forbidden value to make sure it is explicitly set in the parfile` |
| **GRHayLib::Gamma_th** | `REAL` | `-1` | thermal Gamma parameter <br> *Range:* `forbidden value to make sure it is explicitly set in the parfile` |
| **GRHayLib::Lorenz_damping_factor** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Damping factor for the generalized Lorenz gauge. Has units of 1/length = 1/M. Typically set this parameter to 1.5/(maximum Delta t on AMR grids). <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **GRHayLib::P_atm** | `REAL` | `-1` | Atmospheric pressure (required) <br> *Range:* `Used to check if anything was given` |
| **GRHayLib::P_max** | `REAL` | `-1` | Maximum temperature allowed (disabled by default) <br> *Range:* `Used to check if anything was given` |
| **GRHayLib::P_min** | `REAL` | `-1` | Minimum temperature allowed (disabled by default) <br> *Range:* `Used to check if anything was given` |
| **GRHayLib::Psi6threshold** | `REAL` | `1e100` | Where Psi^6 > Psi6threshold, we assume we're inside the horizon in the primitives solver, and certain limits are relaxed or imposed <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **GRHayLib::T_atm** | `REAL` | `-1` | Atmospheric temperature (required) <br> *Range:* `Used to check if anything was given` |
| **GRHayLib::T_max** | `REAL` | `-1` | Maximum temperature allowed (optional; table bounds used by default) <br> *Range:* `N/A` |
| **GRHayLib::T_min** | `REAL` | `-1` | Minimum temperature allowed (optional; table bounds used by default) <br> *Range:* `N/A` |
| **GRHayLib::Y_e_atm** | `REAL` | `-1` | Atmospheric electron fraction (required) <br> *Range:* `Used to check if anything was given` |
| **GRHayLib::Y_e_max** | `REAL` | `-1` | Atmospheric electron fraction (optional; table bounds used by default) <br> *Range:* `N/A` |
| **GRHayLib::Y_e_min** | `REAL` | `-1` | Atmospheric electron fraction (optional; table bounds used by default) <br> *Range:* `N/A` |
| **GRHayLib::calc_primitive_guess** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Do we want to calculate estimates for the primitives in Con2Prim? <br> *Range:* `Calculates primitive guesses from the current conservatives.` |
| **GRHayLib::con2prim_backup_routines** | `KEYWORD` | `None` | We allow up to 3 backup routines <br> *Range:* `https://escholarship.org/content/qt0s53f84b/qt0s53f84b.pdf (see also https://arxiv.org/pdf/1712.07538.pdf)` |
| **GRHayLib::con2prim_routine** | `KEYWORD` | `Noble2D` | What con2prim routine is used <br> *Range:* `https://escholarship.org/content/qt0s53f84b/qt0s53f84b.pdf (see also https://arxiv.org/pdf/1712.07538.pdf)` |
| **GRHayLib::evolve_entropy** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Do we want to evolve the entropy? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHayLib::evolve_temperature** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Do we want to evolve the temperature? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHayLib::k_ppoly0** | `REAL` | `-1` | Also known as k\_ppoly[0], this is the polytropic constant for the lowest density piece of the piecewise polytrope. All other k\_ppoly elements are set using rho\_ppoly\_in and enforcing continuity in the equation of state. <br> *Range:* `forbidden value to make sure it is explicitly set in the parfile` |
| **GRHayLib::max_Lorentz_factor** | `REAL` | `10.0` | Maximum Lorentz factor. <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **GRHayLib::neos** | `INT` | `1` | number of parameters in EOS table <br> *Range:* `1:10` |
| **GRHayLib::ppm_flattening_epsilon** | `REAL` | `0.33` | Epsilon used for flattening in PPM algorithm <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHayLib::ppm_flattening_omega1** | `REAL` | `0.75` | Omega1 used for flattening in PPM algorithm <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHayLib::ppm_flattening_omega2** | `REAL` | `10.0` | Omega2 used for flattening in PPM algorithm <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHayLib::ppm_shock_epsilon** | `REAL` | `0.01` | Epsilon used for shock detection in steepen\_rho algorithm <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHayLib::ppm_shock_eta1** | `REAL` | `20.0` | Eta1 used for shock detection in steepen\_rho algorithm <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHayLib::ppm_shock_eta2** | `REAL` | `0.05` | Eta2 used for shock detection in steepen\_rho algorithm <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHayLib::ppm_shock_k0** | `REAL` | `0.1` | K0 used for shock detection in steepen\_rho algorithm <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHayLib::rho_b_atm** | `REAL` | `-1` | Atmosphere value on the baryonic rest mass density rho\_b (required). If this is unset, the run will die. <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHayLib::rho_b_max** | `REAL` | `-1` | Ceiling value on the baryonic rest mass density rho\_b (disabled by default). Can help with stability to limit densities inside the BH after black hole formation. <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHayLib::rho_b_min** | `REAL` | `-1` | Floor value on the baryonic rest mass density rho\_b (disabled by default). <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHayLib::rho_ppoly_in** | `REAL` | `-1` | Set polytropic rho parameters <br> *Range:* `forbidden value to make sure it is explicitly set in the parfile (only checked for neos > 1)` |

## 📦 Thorn: GRHydro<a id='grhydro'></a><a href='Cactus_Parameters.md#contents' style='float: right;'>🏠</a>
| Name of Parameter | Type | Default | Description / Range |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **GRHydro::Avec_gauge** | `KEYWORD` | `lorenz` | Which gauge condition to use when evolving the vector potential <br> *Range:* `Lorenz gauge` |
| **GRHydro::Check_Rho_Minimum** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should a check on rho < GRHydro\_rho\_min be performed and written as WARNING level 2? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::EoS_Change** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Recalculate fluid quantities if changing the EoS <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::EoS_Change_type** | `KEYWORD` | `Gamma` | Change polytropic K or Gamma? <br> *Range:* `Change K and Gamma, Shibata et al. 2004 3-D GR Core Collapse style` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_MaxNumConstrainedVars** | `INT` | `37` | The maximum number of constrained variables used by GRHydro <br> *Range:* `7:48` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_MaxNumEvolvedVars** | `INT` | `5` | The maximum number of evolved variables used by GRHydro <br> *Range:* `5:12` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_MaxNumEvolvedVarsSlow** | `INT` | `0` | The maximum number of evolved variables used by GRHydro <br> *Range:* `5:12` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_MaxNumSandRVars** | `INT` | `16` | The maximum number of save and restore variables used by GRHydro <br> *Range:* `0:16` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_NaN_verbose** | `INT` | `2` | The warning level for NaNs occuring within GRHydro <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_Y_e_max** | `REAL` | `1.0` | maximum allowed Y\_e <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_Y_e_min** | `REAL` | `0.0` | minimum allowed Y\_e <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_atmo_tolerance** | `REAL` | `0.0` | A point is set to atmosphere in the Con2Prim's if its rho < GRHydro\_rho\_min *(1+GRHydro\_atmo\_tolerance). This avoids occasional spurious oscillations in carpet buffer zones lying in the atmosphere (because prolongation happens on conserved variables) <br> *Range:* `0.0:` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_c2p_failed_action** | `KEYWORD` | `abort` | what to do when we detect a c2p failure <br> *Range:* `request termination` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_c2p_reset_eps_tau_hot_eos** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | As a last resort, reset tau <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_c2p_warn_from_reflevel** | `INT` | `0` | Start warning on given refinement level and on higher levels <br> *Range:* `0:` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_c2p_warnlevel** | `INT` | `0` | Warnlevel for Con2Prim warnings <br> *Range:* `0:1` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_countmax** | `INT` | `100` | Maximum number of iterations for Con2Prim solve <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_countmin** | `INT` | `1` | Minimum number of iterations for Con2Prim solve <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_del_ptol** | `REAL` | `1.e-18` | Tolerance for primitive variable solve (absolute) <br> *Range:* `0:` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_enable_internal_excision** | `BOOLEAN` | `true` | Set this to 'false' to disable the thorn-internal excision. <br> *Range:* `true` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_eos_hot_eps_fix** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Activate quasi-failsafe mode for hot EOSs <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_eos_hot_prim2con_warn** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Warn about temperature workaround in prim2con <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_eos_rf_prec** | `REAL` | `1.0e-8` | Precision to which root finding should be carried out <br> *Range:* `(0.0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_eos_table** | `STRING` | `Ideal_Fluid` | Name for the Equation of State <br> *Range:* `.*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_eos_type** | `KEYWORD` | `General` | Type of Equation of State <br> *Range:* `P = P(rho, eps)` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_eps_min** | `REAL` | `1.e-10` | Minimum value of specific internal energy - this is now only used in GRHydro\_InitData's GRHydro\_Only\_Atmo routine <br> *Range:* `0:` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_hot_atmo_Y_e** | `REAL` | `0.5e0` | Y\_e of the hot atmosphere <br> *Range:* `0.0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_hot_atmo_temp** | `REAL` | `0.1e0` | Temperature of the hot atmosphere in MeV <br> *Range:* `(0.0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_hydro_excision** | `INT` | `1` | Turns excision automatically on in HydroBase <br> *Range:* `1:1` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_lorentz_overshoot_cutoff** | `REAL` | `1.e100` | Set the Lorentz factor to this value in case it overshoots (1/0) <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_max_temp** | `REAL` | `90.0e0` | maximum temperature we allow <br> *Range:* `(0.0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_oppm_reflevel** | `INT` | `-1` | Ref level where oPPM is used instead of ePPM (used with use\_enhaced\_ppm=yes). <br> *Range:* `-1:10` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_perc_ptol** | `REAL` | `1.e-8` | Tolerance for primitive variable solve (percent) <br> *Range:* `0:` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_polish** | `INT` | `0` | Number of extra iterations after root found <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_rho_central** | `REAL` | `1.e-5` | Central Density for Star <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHydro::GRHydro_stencil** | `INT` | `2` | Width of the stencil <br> *Range:* `0:` |
| **GRHydro::HLLE_type** | `KEYWORD` | `Standard` | Which HLLE type to use <br> *Range:* `Tadmor's simplification of HLLE` |
| **GRHydro::Tmunu_damping_radius_max** | `REAL` | `-1` | damping radius at which Tmunu becomes 0 <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::Tmunu_damping_radius_min** | `REAL` | `-1` | damping radius at which we start to damp with a tanh function <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::WENO_order** | `INT` | `5` | The order of accuracy of the WENO reconstruction <br> *Range:* `Fifth-order` |
| **GRHydro::apply_H_viscosity** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | H viscosity is useful to fix the carbuncle instability seen at strong shocks <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::atmo_falloff_power** | `REAL` | `0.0` | The power at which the atmosphere level falls off as (atmo\_falloof\_radius/r)**atmo\_falloff\_power <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::atmo_falloff_radius** | `REAL` | `50.0` | The radius for which the atmosphere starts to falloff as (atmo\_falloff\_radius/r)**atmo\_falloff\_power <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::atmo_tolerance_power** | `REAL` | `0.0` | The power at which the atmosphere tolerance increases as (r/atmo\_tolerance\_radius)**atmo\_tolerance\_power <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::atmo_tolerance_radius** | `REAL` | `50.0` | The radius for which the atmosphere tolerance starts to increase as (r/atmo\_tolerance\_radius)**atmo\_tolerance\_power <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::bound** | `KEYWORD` | `none` | Which boundary condition to use - FIXME <br> *Range:* `Constant` |
| **GRHydro::c2p_reset_pressure** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | If the pressure guess is unphysical should we recompute it? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::c2p_reset_pressure_to_value** | `REAL` | `1.e-20` | The value to which the pressure is reset to when a failure occurrs in C2P <br> *Range:* `0:` |
| **GRHydro::c2p_resort_to_bisection** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | If the pressure guess is unphysical, should we try with bisection (slower, but more robust) <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::calculate_bcom** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Calculate the comoving contravariant magnetic 4-vector b^a? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::check_for_trivial_rp** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Do check for trivial Riemann Problem <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **GRHydro::clean_divergence** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use hyperbolic divergence cleaning <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::comoving_attenuate** | `KEYWORD` | `tanh` | Which attenuation method for the comoving shift <br> *Range:* `Multiply by 1/2*(1+tanh(factor*(rho/rho_max - offset)))` |
| **GRHydro::comoving_factor** | `REAL` | `0.0` | Factor multiplying the velocity for the comoving shift <br> *Range:* `0.0:2.0` |
| **GRHydro::comoving_tanh_factor** | `REAL` | `10.0` | The factor in the above tanh attenuation <br> *Range:* `(0.0:*` |
| **GRHydro::comoving_tanh_offset** | `REAL` | `0.05` | The offset in the above tanh attenuation <br> *Range:* `0.0:1.0` |
| **GRHydro::comoving_v_method** | `KEYWORD` | `projected` | Which method for getting the radial velocity <br> *Range:* `vr = sqrt(v_i v^i)` |
| **GRHydro::con2prim_oct_hack** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Disregard c2p failures in oct/rotsym90 boundary cells with xyz < 0 <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::decouple_normal_Bfield** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | when using divergence cleaning properly decouple Bx,psidc subsystem <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **GRHydro::enhanced_ppm_C2** | `REAL` | `1.25` | Parameter for enhancecd ppm limiter. Default from McCorquodale & Colella 2011 <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **GRHydro::enhanced_ppm_C3** | `REAL` | `0.1` | Parameter for enhancecd ppm limiter. Default from McCorquodale & Colella 2011 <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::eno_order** | `INT` | `2` | The order of accuracy of the ENO reconstruction <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **GRHydro::evolve_tracer** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should we advect tracers? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::gradient_method** | `KEYWORD` | `First diff` | Which method is used to set GRHydro::DiffRho? <br> *Range:* `Based on the size of rho` |
| **GRHydro::initial_Gamma** | `REAL` | `1.3333` | If changing Gamma, what was the value used in the initial data routine? <br> *Range:* `(0.0:` |
| **GRHydro::initial_atmosphere_factor** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | A relative (to the initial atmosphere) value for rho in the atmosphere. This is used at initial time only. Unused if negative. <br> *Range:* `-1.0:` |
| **GRHydro::initial_k** | `REAL` | `100.0` | If changing K, what was the value used in the initial data routine? <br> *Range:* `(0.0:` |
| **GRHydro::initial_rho_abs_min** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | An absolute value for rho in the atmosphere. To be used by initial data routines only. Unused if negative. <br> *Range:* `-1.0:` |
| **GRHydro::initial_rho_rel_min** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | A relative (to the central density) value for rho in the atmosphere. To be used by initial data routines only. Unused if negative. <br> *Range:* `-1.0:` |
| **GRHydro::kap_dc** | `REAL` | `10.0` | The kap parameter for divergence cleaning <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::left_eigenvectors** | `KEYWORD` | `analytical` | How to compute the left eigenvectors <br> *Range:* `Numerical left eigenvectors` |
| **GRHydro::max_magnetic_to_gas_pressure_ratio** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | consider pressure to be magnetically dominated if magnetic pressure to gas pressure ratio is higher than this <br> *Range:* `disable` |
| **GRHydro::method_type** | `KEYWORD` | `RSA FV` | Which type of method to use <br> *Range:* `Finite difference using Lax-Friedrichs flux splitting` |
| **GRHydro::min_tracer** | `REAL` | `0.0` | The floor placed on the tracer <br> *Range:* `*:*` |
| **GRHydro::mp5_adaptive_eps** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Same as WENO adaptive epsilon: adaptively reduce mp5\_eps according to norm of stencil. Original algorithm does not use this. <br> *Range:* `Same as WENO adaptive epsilon: adaptively reduce mp5_eps according to norm of stencil. Original algorithm does not use this.` |
| **GRHydro::mp5_alpha** | `REAL` | `4.0` | alpha parameter for MP5 reconstruction <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::mp5_eps** | `REAL` | `0.0` | epsilon parameter for MP5 reconstruction <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::myfloor** | `REAL` | `1.e-10` | A minimum number for the TVD reconstruction routine <br> *Range:* `0.0:` |
| **GRHydro::number_of_arrays** | `INT` | `0` | Number of arrays to evolve <br> *Range:* `0:3` |
| **GRHydro::number_of_particles** | `INT` | `0` | Number of particles to track <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::number_of_tracers** | `INT` | `0` | Number of tracer variables to be advected <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::numerical_viscosity** | `KEYWORD` | `fast` | How to compute the numerical viscosity <br> *Range:* `Normal numerical viscosity` |
| **GRHydro::particle_interpolation_order** | `INT` | `2` | What order should be used for the particle interpolation <br> *Range:* `1:*` |
| **GRHydro::particle_interpolator** | `STRING` | `Lagrange polynomial interpolation` | What interpolator should be used for the particles <br> *Range:* `A valid interpolator name` |
| **GRHydro::ppm_detect** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should the PPM solver use shock detection? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::ppm_epsilon** | `REAL` | `0.33` | Epsilon for PPM zone flattening <br> *Range:* `0.0:` |
| **GRHydro::ppm_epsilon_shock** | `REAL` | `0.01` | Epsilon for PPM shock detection <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHydro::ppm_eta1** | `REAL` | `20.0` | Eta1 for PPM shock detection <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHydro::ppm_eta2** | `REAL` | `0.05` | Eta2 for PPM shock detection <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHydro::ppm_flatten** | `KEYWORD` | `stencil_3` | Which flattening procedure should the PPM solver use? <br> *Range:* `original C&W flattening procedure, which requires stencil 4` |
| **GRHydro::ppm_k0** | `REAL` | `0.2` | K0 for PPM shock detection <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHydro::ppm_mppm** | `INT` | `0` | Use modified (enhanced) ppm scheme <br> *Range:* `0:1` |
| **GRHydro::ppm_mppm_debug_eigenvalues** | `INT` | `0` | write eigenvalues into debug grid variables <br> *Range:* `0:1` |
| **GRHydro::ppm_omega1** | `REAL` | `0.75` | Omega1 for PPM zone flattening <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHydro::ppm_omega2** | `REAL` | `10.0` | Omega2 for PPM zone flattening <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHydro::ppm_omega_tracer** | `REAL` | `0.50` | Omega for tracer PPM zone flattening <br> *Range:* `:` |
| **GRHydro::ppm_small** | `REAL` | `1.e-7` | A floor used by PPM shock detection <br> *Range:* `0.0:1.0` |
| **GRHydro::psidcspeed** | `KEYWORD` | `light speed` | Which speed to set for psidc <br> *Range:* `Manually set the characteristic speeds: [setcharmin,setcharmax]` |
| **GRHydro::recon_method** | `KEYWORD` | `tvd` | Which reconstruction method to use <br> *Range:* `MP5 reconstruction` |
| **GRHydro::recon_vars** | `KEYWORD` | `primitive` | Which type of variables to reconstruct <br> *Range:* `Reconstruct the conserved variables` |
| **GRHydro::reconstruct_Wv** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Reconstruct the primitive velocity W\_Lorentz*vel, rather than just vel. <br> *Range:* `Reconstruct the primitive velocity W_Lorentz*vel, rather than just vel.` |
| **GRHydro::reconstruct_temper** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | if set to true, temperature will be reconstructed <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::rho_abs_min** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | A minimum rho below which evolution is turned off (atmosphere). If negative, the relative minimum will be used instead. <br> *Range:* `-1.0:` |
| **GRHydro::rho_abs_min_after_recovery** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | A new absolute value for rho in the atmosphere. To be used after recovering. Unused if negative. <br> *Range:* `-1.0:` |
| **GRHydro::rho_rel_min** | `REAL` | `1.e-9` | A minimum relative rho (rhomin = centden * rho\_rel\_min) below which evolution is turned off (atmosphere). Only used if rho\_abs\_min < 0.0 <br> *Range:* `0:` |
| **GRHydro::riemann_solver** | `KEYWORD` | `HLLE` | Which Riemann solver to use <br> *Range:* `Local Lax-Friedrichs (MHD only at the moment)` |
| **GRHydro::set_trivial_rp_grid_function** | `INT` | `0` | set gf for triv. rp (only for debugging) <br> *Range:* `0:1` |
| **GRHydro::setcharmax** | `REAL` | `1.0` | Maximum characteristic speed for psidc if psidcspeed is set <br> *Range:* `0:1` |
| **GRHydro::setcharmin** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | Minimum characteristic speed for psidc if psidcspeed is set <br> *Range:* `-1:0` |
| **GRHydro::sources_spatial_order** | `INT` | `2` | Order of spatial differencing of the source terms <br> *Range:* `4th order finite differencing` |
| **GRHydro::sqrtdet_thr** | `REAL` | `-1.0` | Threshold to apply cons rescalings deep inside the horizon <br> *Range:* `Do not apply limit` |
| **GRHydro::sync_conserved_only** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Only sync evolved conserved quantities during evolution. <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::tau_rel_min** | `REAL` | `1.e-10` | A minimum relative tau (taumin = maxtau(t=0) * tau\_rel\_min) below which tau is reschaled <br> *Range:* `0:` |
| **GRHydro::track_divB** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Track the magnetic field constraint violations <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::transport_constraints** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use constraint transport for magnetic field <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::tvd_limiter** | `KEYWORD` | `minmod` | Which slope limiter to use <br> *Range:* `Superbee` |
| **GRHydro::use_MoL_slow_multirate_sector** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Whether to make use of MoL's slow multirate sector <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::use_enhanced_ppm** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use the enhanced ppm reconstruction method proposed by Colella & Sekora 2008 and McCorquodale & Colella 2011 <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::use_evolution_mask** | `KEYWORD` | `never` | Set this to 'always' to skip validity tests in regions where CarpetEvolutionMask::evolution\_mask vanishes. <br> *Range:* `do not use the mask` |
| **GRHydro::use_min_tracer** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Should there be a floor on the tracer? <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::use_optimized_ppm** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | use C++ templated version of PPM. Experimental <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::use_weighted_fluxes** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Weight the flux terms by the cell surface areas <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::weno_adaptive_epsilon** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | use modified smoothness indicators that take into account scale of solution (adaptive epsilon) <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **GRHydro::weno_eps** | `REAL` | `1e-26` | WENO epsilon parameter. For WENO-z, 1e-40 is recommended <br> *Range:* `0:*` |
| **GRHydro::wk_atmosphere** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Use some of Wolfgang Kastauns atmosphere tricks <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::constrain_to_1D** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Set fluid velocities to zero for non-radial motion <br> *Range:* `no` |
| **GRHydro::use_cxx_code** | `BOOLEAN` | `yes` | Use experimental C++ code? <br> *Range:* `yes` |
| **GRHydro::verbose** | `BOOLEAN` | `no` | Some debug output <br> *Range:* `no` |
